132	  console.log(`dump names: ${DUMPNAME.size} (8ch dir=${G2})`);
133	  const rows: Row[] = [];
134	  let curStore: Parameters<typeof cmp>[2] | null = null;
135	  const seen = new Set<number>();
136	  const unmapped = new Set<string>();
137	  const RNGp = RNG.prototype as unknown as Record<string, unknown>;
138	  const oReseed = RNGp.reseed as (this: unknown, s: number) => void;
139	  RNGp.reseed = function (this: unknown, s: number) {
140	    if (curStore) {
141	      const stack = new Error().stack ?? '';
142	      for (const line of stack.split('\n')) {
143	        const m = line.match(/((?:\/|[\w./-]*)src\/[^\s()]+\.ts):(\d+):\d+/);
144	        if (m && !m[1].includes('/core/rng') && !m[1].includes('_mile8')) {
145	          const lab = labelOf(m[1], Number(m[2]));
146	          const idx = LABEL2DUMP.get(lab);
147	          if (idx !== undefined && !seen.has(idx)) {
148	            seen.add(idx);
149	            rows.push(cmp(lab, idx, curStore!));
150	          } else if (idx === undefined && !unmapped.has(lab)) {
151	            unmapped.add(lab);
152	            console.log(`  [unmapped-label] ${lab} @${m[1].replace(/^.*?(src\/)/, '$1')}:${m[2]}`);
153	          }
154	          break;
155	        }
156	      }
157	    }
158	    return oReseed.call(this, s);
159	  };
160	  const t0 = Date.now();
161	  try {
162	    try {
163	      await generateWorld({
164	        width: W, height: H, seedText: SEED, evil: EVIL as -1 | 0 | 1,
165	        onWorldPartial: (w, i) => {
166	          if (!curStore) curStore = w.store as never as typeof curStore;
167	          const idx = SLOT_END.get(i);
168	          if (idx === undefined || seen.has(idx)) return;
169	          seen.add(idx);
170	          rows.push(cmp(`槽末#${i}`, idx, w.store as never as typeof curStore));
171	        },
172	      });
173	    } catch (e) { throw e; }
174	  } finally { RNGp.reseed = oReseed; }
175	  rows.sort((a, b) => a.idx - b.idx);
176	  for (const r of rows) {
177	    const bad = r.dA || r.dT || r.dW || r.dL || r.dHf || r.dSl || r.dLt || r.dIa;
178	    console.log(`${String(r.idx).padStart(3)} ${DUMPNAME.get(r.idx) ?? '?'} ← ${r.tag.padEnd(22)} ` +
179	      `A=${String(r.dA).padStart(7)} T=${String(r.dT).padStart(7)} W=${String(r.dW).padStart(6)} L=${String(r.dL).padStart(6)} ` +
180	      `Hf=${String(r.dHf).padStart(6)} Sl=${String(r.dSl).padStart(6)} Lt=${String(r.dLt).padStart(6)} Ia=${String(r.dIa).padStart(4)} (Gh=${r.dGh}) ` +
181	      `${bad ? '✗ ' + r.first : '✓'}`);
182	    if (bad) {
183	      console.log(`     pairs: ${r.pairs.map(([k, n]) => `${k}×${n}`).join(' ')}`);
184	      console.log(`     samples: ${r.samples.join(' | ')}`);
185	    }
186	  }
187	  const firstBad = rows.find((r) => r.dA || r.dT || r.dW || r.dL || r.dHf || r.dSl || r.dLt || r.dIa);
188	  const firstGh = rows.find((r) => r.dGh);
189	  console.log(`\n★ 8通道首差边界: ${firstBad ? `#${String(firstBad.idx).padStart(3)} ${DUMPNAME.get(firstBad.idx)} @${firstBad.tag} ${firstBad.first} ${firstBad.pairs.slice(0, 4).map(([k, n]) => `${k}×${n}`).join(' ')}` : '无（0..31 全绿）'}`);
190	  console.log(`★ ghost 首差边界(信息位): ${firstGh ? `#${firstGh.idx} ${DUMPNAME.get(firstGh.idx)} gh=${firstGh.dGh}` : '无'}`);
191	  console.log(`done ${((Date.now() - t0) / 1000).toFixed(0)}s`);
192	  writeFileSync('/tmp/mile8.json', JSON.stringify(rows));
193	}, 900_000);
194	

```

## JJJJ 批（2026-08-18）：第二种子 12345 里程碑 dump 链（x86 106 槽×8 通道）+ 双种子全链对拍

**任务**：为 seed 12345 生成 x86 槽 dump 链，跑全链里程碑对拍，把"单种子绿"升级为
"双种子绿"；产出 12345 种子债清单（通用债 vs 种子特异债对照）。

### ⓪ rig（可复用文档：第二种子 8 通道真值链生产配方）

- **谱系**：`/tmp/sw-slp/TerrariaServer.hs.exe`（GGQ 批 WriteGrid 改道 ProbeLib
  `HsProbe.WriteGrid` 的 dumpall exe，md5 80e7ca0e…）+ `/tmp/sw-slp/Terraria.app`
  私有克隆 bundle（Resources 内 ProbeLib.dll 同步部署）。起跑前 md5 复核
  Resources/TerrariaServer.exe == hs.exe（历史上 dgl3/dgl4 织入版会残留）。
- **命令**（端口 7794、guard 仿 gen86.sh、HOME 独立副本防并行会话互踩）：
  ```sh
  mkdir -p /tmp/sw-slp/g12345 && rm -f /tmp/sw-slp/home12345/g12345.wld   # 旧 wld 在=载入不生成
  cd /tmp/sw-slp/Terraria.app/Contents/MacOS && \
  HOME=/tmp/sw-slp/home12345 SW_DUMP_ALL=/tmp/sw-slp/g12345 SW_HS_DUMP=1 \
  SW_PASSCHAIN=/tmp/sw-slp/g12345/pc.txt SW_EVIL=1 \
  nohup arch -x86_64 ./TerrariaServer.bin.osx -autocreate 1 -worldname w -seed 12345 \
    -world /tmp/sw-slp/home12345/g12345.wld -port 7794 -noupnp -difficulty 0 \
    </dev/null > /tmp/sw-slp/g12345/run.log 2>&1 &
  # 轮询 grep -c '^P|' pc.txt 到 106 → Server started → kill 本 PID + 等 7794 清零
  ```
  （**必须 x86 切片**：arm64 与 x86 有平台浮点噪声；`-autocreate 1`=4200×1200 勿用 3）。
- **evil 取值依据**：12345 自然 evil=**crimson**（s12345.wld 解析 isCrimson=1，Evil:-1
  自然跑）；AAAA/FFFF 全链基线即此 wld，故 `SW_EVIL=1` + JS `evil:1`（覆写不掷流，
  _matrixdump 已证「骰照掷仅覆盖结果」）。注意 golden `12345世界.wld` 是 **corruption**
  版（evil0），与 x86 矩阵基线是两个世界，勿混。

  + `run.log`，共 5.0G；`/tmp/sw-slp/home12345/g12345.wld` = 同流终态世界。
- **mile8 旁路参**（本批唯一源码级新增，默认行为不变）：

517		public static void MakeDungeon_GenerateNextEntranceHall_Precalculated(DungeonData data, UnifiedRandom genRand, double dist, Vector2D entrancePos, ref int amountPassed, ref Vector2D currentPos)
518		{
519			//IL_0024: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)

527			int num = genRand.Next(10, 30);

532			Vector2D val = Vector2D.Lerp(currentPos, entrancePos, (double)amountPassed / dist);

539				UsePrecalculatedEntrance = true

752		public static DungeonHallSettings MakeDungeon_GetHallSettings(DungeonHallType hallType, DungeonData data, Vector2 hallStart, Vector2 hallEnd, DungeonGenerationStyleData style)
753		{
754			UnifiedRandom genRand = WorldGen.genRand;
755			DungeonHallSettings dungeonHallSettings = null;
756			switch (hallType)
757			{
758			default:
759				dungeonHallSettings = new LegacyDungeonHallSettings();
760				break;
761			case DungeonHallType.Regular:
762				dungeonHallSettings = new RegularDungeonHallSettings();
763				break;
764			case DungeonHallType.Stairwell:
765				dungeonHallSettings = new StairwellDungeonHallSettings
766				{
767					CrackedBrickChance = 0.0
768				};
769				break;
770			case DungeonHallType.Sine:
771			{
772				int num = Math.Max(1, (int)((hallStart - hallEnd).Length() / 30f));
773				int iterations = ((num <= 1) ? 1 : (1 + genRand.Next(num - 1)));
774				float magnitude = 8f + genRand.NextFloat() * 4f;
775				dungeonHallSettings = new SineDungeonHallSettings
776				{
777					CrackedBrickChance = 0.0,
778					Magnitude = magnitude,
779					Iterations = iterations,
780					FlipSine = (genRand.Next(2) == 0)
781				};
782				break;
783			}
784			}
785			dungeonHallSettings.RandomSeed = genRand.Next();
786			dungeonHallSettings.HallType = hallType;
787			dungeonHallSettings.StyleData = style;
788			return dungeonHallSettings;
789		}
790

792		{

794			dungeonEntranceSettings.RandomSeed = preSettings.RandomSeed;
795			return dungeonEntranceSettings;
796		}

805				{

807					StyleData = styleData

809				int num = 0;

812					num += 30;
813				}
814				switch (entranceType)
815				{
816				default:
817					preGenDungeonEntranceSettings.BuriedEntranceYOffset = num;
818					preGenDungeonEntranceSettings.BuriedEntranceSandDugoutYOffset = -num;
819					preGenDungeonEntranceSettings.RoughHeight = 40;
820					break;
821				case DungeonEntranceType.Dome:
822					preGenDungeonEntranceSettings.PrecalculateEntrancePosition = true;
823					preGenDungeonEntranceSettings.BuriedEntranceYOffset = 20 + num;
824					preGenDungeonEntranceSettings.BuriedEntranceSandDugoutYOffset = -num;
825					preGenDungeonEntranceSettings.RoughHeight = 55;
826					break;
827				case DungeonEntranceType.Tower:
828					preGenDungeonEntranceSettings.PrecalculateEntrancePosition = true;
829					preGenDungeonEntranceSettings.BuriedEntranceYOffset = 20 + num;
830					preGenDungeonEntranceSettings.BuriedEntranceSandDugoutYOffset = -num;
831					preGenDungeonEntranceSettings.RoughHeight = 120;
832					break;
833				}
834				preGenDungeonEntranceSettings.RandomSeed = genRand.Next();
835				return preGenDungeonEntranceSettings;
836			}
837			bool flag = false;
838			switch (entranceType)
839			{
840			default:
841				dungeonEntranceSettings = new LegacyDungeonEntranceSettings();
842				break;
843			case DungeonEntranceType.Dome:
844				dungeonEntranceSettings = new DomeDungeonEntranceSettings();
845				dungeonEntranceSettings.PrecalculateEntrancePosition = true;
846				break;
847			case DungeonEntranceType.Tower:
848				dungeonEntranceSettings = new TowerDungeonEntranceSettings();
849				dungeonEntranceSettings.PrecalculateEntrancePosition = true;
850				break;
851			}
852			dungeonEntranceSettings.RandomSeed = genRand.Next();
853			dungeonEntranceSettings.EntranceType = entranceType;
854			if (!flag)
855			{
856				dungeonEntranceSettings.StyleData = styleData;
857			}
858			return dungeonEntranceSettings;
859		}

I see — `dgPillarStrip` doesn't have an "actuated" parameter. The vanilla `GenerateTileStrip` sets `tile.inActive(true)` when `flag4` (PillarType is BlockActuated family). For the Dome's pillar① (BlockActuated), all placed tiles get inActive=true. The JS's dgPillar/dgPillarStrip never sets wire bit5 → Ia=234 missing.

Fix: add an `act` (inActive) parameter to dgPillarStrip/dgPillar that sets `st.wire[ti] |= 32` (bit5). Note vanilla condition: `(!flag2 || j >= num4+2) && (!flag3 || j <= num5-2) && flag4` — flag2/flag3 are SolidTop/SolidBottom variants (false for plain BlockActuated), so with flag4=true all cells get inActive. For the Dome pillars: PillarType.BlockActuated → all cells of the strip (including crowns, which also use the same pillarSettings — the crown strips call GenerateTileStrip with the same settings, so crowns are actuated too).

Wait — the crown strips in `Pillar`: `GenerateTileStrip(dungeonPillarSettings, upwards: true, out topY, out topY, num2 - 1, topY2 + num, pillarHeight, ...)` — same dungeonPillarSettings → flag4 true → crown tiles actuated too.

234 cells: two pillars. Each pillar: 3 main columns full height + 2 crown columns. Let me count later — just fix and measure.

Now, in JS the wire array: `st.wire[i] >>> 5 & 1` is the inact bit (from mile8 cmp: `const jia = (st.wire[i]! >>> 5) & 1;`). So bit5 = inActive. I need to check how DungeonPass sets st.wire elsewhere (e.g., dgSwitch, traps wires) to follow the same style.

 "content": "// 一次性诊断（MMMM 批 2026-08-18）：12345 链 #32 槽（Dungeon pass 末态）分簇对拍。\n// 捕获点 = ▶MountainCaveOpenings reseed 时（= 032_Dungeon dump 时点），随即中止生成。\n// 分簇：T/W 对 (v→j) 计数+首差坐标（穹壳盒内外分开）、Sl 全列、Ia 列直方图。\n// 用法：cd game && SW_MMMM32=1 npx vitest run tools/_mmmm32.test.ts\nimport { it } from 'vitest';\nimport { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';\nimport { generateWorld } from '../src/world/gen/WorldGen';\nimport { TILE_DEFS } from '../src/data/tiles';\nimport { RNG } from '../src/core/rng';\n\nconst RUN = process.env.SW_MMMM32 === '1';\nconst G2 = '/tmp/sw-slp/g12345';\nconst SEED = '12345', EVIL = 1;\nconst W = 4200, H = 1200, N = W * H;\nconst SHEET = new Int32Array(TILE_DEFS.length).fill(-1);\nfor (let i = 0; i < TILE_DEFS.length; i++) {\n  const s = TILE_DEFS[i]?.vanilla?.sheet;\n  if (typeof s === 'number') SHEET[i] = s;\n}\nconst le16 = (p: string): Uint16Array => {\n  const b = readFileSync(p);\n  return new Uint16Array(b.buffer, b.byteOffset, b.length >> 1);\n};\nconst pre = `${G2}/032_Dungeon`;\nconst cT = le16(`${pre}.typ`), cA = readFileSync(`${pre}.act`), cW = le16(`${pre}.wal`),\n  cHf = readFileSync(`${pre}.half`), cSl = readFileSync(`${pre}.slope`), cIa = readFileSync(`${pre}.inact`);\n\ninterface Store {\n  type: Uint16Array; flags: Uint8Array; wall: Uint16Array; liquid: Uint8Array;\n  liquidType: Uint8Array; half: Uint8Array; slope: Uint8Array; wire: Uint8Array;\n}\n\n(RUN ? it : it.skip)('MMMM #32 cluster diff', async () => {\n  let captured: Store | null = null;\n  const abort = Symbol('abort32');\n  const RNGp = RNG.prototype as unknown as Record<string, unknown>;\n  const oReseed = RNGp.reseed as (this: unknown, s: number) => void;\n  RNGp.reseed = function (this: unknown, s: number) {\n    const stack = new Error().stack ?? '';\n    if (/▶MountainCaveOpenings/.test(stack)) {\n      throw abort;\n    }\n    return oReseed.call(this, s);\n  };\n  try {\n    await generateWorld({ width: W, height: H, seedText: SEED, evil: EVIL as -1 | 0 | 1 });\n  } catch (e) {\n    if (e !== abort) throw e;\n  } finally { RNGp.reseed = oReseed; }\n  if (!captured) throw new Error('capture missed');\n  const st = captured;\n  // 穹壳盒（i=3413,n7=190 → x[3373,3453] y[150,199]；宽松取 x±45,y±45 判内外）\n  const cx = 3413, cy = 190;\n  const inDome = (x: number, y: number): string =>\n    (Math.abs(x - cx) <= 45 && Math.abs(y - cy) <= 45) ? 'D' : 'O';\n  const tPairs = new Map<string, { n: number; xs: number[] }>();\n  const wPairs = new Map<string, { n: number; xs: number[] }>();\n  const slDiff: string[] = [];\n  const iaCols = new Map<number, { a: number; b: number }>();\n  let dA = 0, dT = 0, dW = 0, dHf = 0, dSl = 0, dIa = 0;\n  const bump = (m: Map<string, { n: number; xs: number[] }>, k: string, x: number, y: number) => {\n    let e = m.get(k);\n    if (!e) { e = { n: 0, xs: [] }; m.set(k, e); }\n    e.n++;\n    if (e.xs.length < 30) e.xs.push(x, y);\n  };\n  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {\n    const x = i % W, y = (i / W) | 0;\n    const va = cA[i]!, ja = st.flags[i]! & 1;\n    if (va !== ja) { dA++; }\n    const vt = va ? cT[i]! : 0;\n    const jt = ja ? (SHEET[st.type[i]!] >= 0 ? SHEET[st.type[i]!]! : 9999) : 0;\n    if (vt !== jt) { dT++; bump(tPairs, `${vt}>${jt}|${inDome(x, y)}`, x, y); }\n    if (cW[i]! !== st.wall[i]!) { dW++; bump(wPairs, `${cW[i]}>${st.wall[i]}|${inDome(x, y)}`, x, y); }\n    if (cHf[i]! !== st.half[i]!) dHf++;\n    if (cSl[i]! !== st.slope[i]!) { dSl++; if (slDiff.length < 40) slDiff.push(`(${x},${y}) v${cSl[i]} j${st.slope[i]}`); }\n    const via = cIa[i]! ? 1 : 0;\n    const jia = (st.wire[i]! >>> 5) & 1;\n    if (via !== jia) {\n      dIa++;\n      const col = iaCols.get(x) ?? { a: 0, b: 0 };\n      if (via) col.a++; else col.b++;\n      iaCols.set(x, col);\n    }\n  }\n  const fmt = (m: Map<string, { n: number; xs: number[] }>) =>\n    [...m.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1].n - a[1].n)\n      .map(([k, e]) => `${k}×${e.n} @${e.xs.filter((_, q) => q % 2 === 0).slice(0, 6).map((vx, q2) => `(${vx},${e.xs[q2 * 2 + 1]})`).join('')}`).join('\\n  ');\n  console.log(`#32 A=${dA} T=${dT} W=${dW} Hf=${dHf} Sl=${dSl} Ia=${dIa}`);\n  console.log(`T pairs (v>j|Dome):\\n  ${fmt(tPairs)}`);\n  console.log(`W pairs (v>j|Dome):\\n  ${fmt(wPairs)}`);\n  console.log(`Sl: ${slDiff.join(' ')}`);\n  console.log(`Ia cols: ${[...iaCols.entries()].sort((a, b) => a[0] - b[0]).map(([x, c]) => `x${x}:v${c.a}/j${c.b}`).join(' ')}`);\n  writeFileSync('/tmp/mmmm32.json', JSON.stringify({\n    t: [...tPairs.entries()].map(([k, e]) => [k, e.n, e.xs]),\n    w: [...wPairs.entries()].map(([k, e]) => [k, e.n, e.xs]),\n    sl: slDiff,\n  }));\n}, 240_000);\n"
}
```

	{
		if (Main.tile[i, j] != null && Main.tile[i, j].active())
		{
			return Main.tile[i, j].inActive();
		}
		return true;
	}

	public static bool SolidTile(int i, int j, bool noDoors = false)

		}

144		private Point Lights_GenerateSwitch(int x, int y)
145		{
146			Point result = Point.Zero;
147			for (int i = 0; i < 1000; i++)
148			{
149				int num = x + WorldGen.genRand.Next(-12, 13);
150				int num2 = y + WorldGen.genRand.Next(3, 21);
151				if (Main.tile[num, num2].active() || Main.tile[num, num2 + 1].active() || !DungeonUtils.IsConsideredDungeonTile(Main.tile[num - 1, num2].type) || !DungeonUtils.IsConsideredDungeonTile(Main.tile[num + 1, num2].type) || !Collision.CanHit(new Point(num * 16, num2 * 16), 16, 16, new Point(x * 16, y * 16 + 1), 16, 16))
152				{
153					continue;
154				}
155				if (((WorldGen.SolidTile(num - 1, num2) && Main.tile[num - 1, num2].type != 10) || (WorldGen.SolidTile(num + 1, num2) && Main.tile[num + 1, num2].type != 10) || WorldGen.SolidTile(num, num2 + 1)) && DungeonUtils.IsConsideredDungeonWall(Main.tile[num, num2].wall) && (DungeonUtils.IsConsideredDungeonTile(Main.tile[num - 1, num2].type) || DungeonUtils.IsConsideredDungeonTile(Main.tile[num + 1, num2].type)))
156				{
157					WorldGen.PlaceTile(num, num2, 136, mute: true);
158					result = new Point(num, num2);
159				}
160				if (!Main.tile[num, num2].active())
161				{
162					continue;
163				}
164				while (num != x || num2 != y)
165				{
166					Main.tile[num, num2].wire(wire: true);
167					if (num > x)
168					{
169						num--;
170					}
171					if (num < x)
172					{
173						num++;
174					}
175					Main.tile[num, num2].wire(wire: true);
176					if (num2 > y)
177					{
178						num2--;
179					}
180					if (num2 < y)
181					{
182						num2++;
183					}
184					Main.tile[num, num2].wire(wire: true);
185				}
186				if (WorldGen.genRand.Next(3) > 0)
187				{
188					Main.tile[x, y].frameX = 18;
189					Main.tile[x, y + 1].frameX = 18;
190				}
191				break;
192			}
193			return result;
194		}
195	}

	public static void GenerateBottomWedge(int placeX, int placeY, int pillarWidth, ushort pillarType, bool left = true, bool wall = false, bool actuated = false, bool crowningBottom = false, int paint = -1)
	{
		if (crowningBottom)
		{
			pillarWidth += 2;
		}
		int topY = 0;
		for (int i = 0; i <= pillarWidth; i++)
		{
			int placeX2 = placeX + i - pillarWidth / 2;
			int pillarHeight = (left ? (i + 1) : (pillarWidth - (i - 1)));
			GenerateTileStrip(upwards: false, out topY, out topY, placeX2, placeY, pillarHeight, pillarType, wall, actuated, paint);
		}
		for (int j = 0; j <= pillarWidth; j++)
		{
			_ = pillarWidth / 2;
			int num = (left ? (j + 1) : (pillarWidth - (j - 1)));
			Tile.SmoothSlope(placeX, placeY + num, applyToNeighbors: false);
		}
	}

import { TILE_DEFS } from '../../../data/tiles';

/** 生成期实心覆写表：内部 tile id → 当前实心值（未登记 = 用 TILE_DEFS 初值）。 */
const solidOverrides = new Map<number, boolean>();

/** sheet id → 内部 tile id 列表（同一 sheet 可能挂多个内部 id,全部翻转） */
const IDS_BY_SHEET = new Map<number, number[]>();
for (let i = 0; i < TILE_DEFS.length; i++) {
  const s = TILE_DEFS[i]?.vanilla?.sheet;
  if (typeof s !== 'number') continue;
  const list = IDS_BY_SHEET.get(s);
  if (list) list.push(i);
  else IDS_BY_SHEET.set(s, [i]);
}

/** 生成开始清零（对应原版 Main.tileSolid 为进程级静态、每局生成重放同一翻转

export function resetGenSolid(): void {
  solidOverrides.clear();

/** 按内部 tile id 查生成期实心值（Main.tileSolid[type] 等价）。 */
export function genSolidType(t: number): boolean {
  const o = solidOverrides.get(t);
  if (o !== undefined) return o;
  return !!TILE_DEFS[t]?.solid;
}

/** 原版 Main.tileSolid=true 而 TILE_DEFS.solid(运行时碰撞)=false 的 9 类 sheet：
 *  19 平台族(Main.cs:7842)/239/380/427/435-439(Echo 族)——vanilla-tilecollision.json
 *  对账差集（这 9 类无生成期翻转记录）。genSolidType() 兜底是 TILE_DEFS.solid
 *  （碰撞语义），凡语义上要读「生成期 Main.tileSolid」的调用方（Pots 首实心门/
 *  PlacePot 地面门、SpreadGrass 3×3 实心扫描等）须经由本包装——尾部清剿批
 *  2026-08-17。 */
const VAN_TILE_SOLID_EXTRA = new Set([19, 239, 380, 427, 435, 436, 437, 438, 439]);
const SHEET_OF_TYPE = new Int32Array(TILE_DEFS.length).fill(-1);
for (let i = 0; i < TILE_DEFS.length; i++) {
  const s = TILE_DEFS[i]?.vanilla?.sheet;
  if (typeof s === 'number') SHEET_OF_TYPE[i] = s;
}
export function vanGenSolidType(t: number): boolean {
  if (VAN_TILE_SOLID_EXTRA.has(SHEET_OF_TYPE[t] ?? -1)) return true;
  return genSolidType(t);
}

/** 生成期实心查询（供消费端替代裸 `TILE_DEFS[t]?.solid`）：
 *  active && 生成期 tileSolid。half/slope/platform 等衍生语义由调用方自行叠加
 *  （各 pass 的 SolidTile 近似口径不一,不在此处收敛）。 */
export function isGenSolid(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean {
  if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return false;
  const i = st.idx(x, y);
  if (!st.flags[i]) return false;
  return genSolidType(st.type[i]);
}

```

FFFF's code `d.nowb === false` only matches when nowb is EXPLICITLY false — but the push omits the field for the lower pair → undefined!

THAT's the remaining drift: entries #6/#7 should consume rn(50) per book-roll-column but don't. Fix: `d.nowb !== true` (or explicitly set nowb: false in the pushes). Cleanest: change the condition to `d.nowb !== true && rn(50) === 0`.

So per book-roll column: rd() (gate) → rn(50) (always, when !NoWaterbolt) → rn(5) (on success). The JS currently: `const wb = d.nowb === false && rn(50) === 0;` — SHORT-CIRCUIT: if nowb is undefined → `undefined === false` = false → rn(50) NOT consumed → 1 roll missing per books-gate column in entries #6/#7!!
